## Ledger: the Regroup launch program (LP-)

- **Checked against:** `/Users/marcusklein/dev/rp-210-merge`, detached at `523c633d` (origin/main per the brief). Run 2026-11-21.
- **Scope:** launch-reboot spec, roadmap, P1/P2/P3a/P4a/P6/fixes plans, staging-operator spec and plan, `regroup/docs/operations/**`, `regroup/docs/launch-readiness/*`.
- **Not done (brief limits):** no deploy-state reads (this task didn't authorize them), no Stripe calls, no production data. Every "is it live / deployed / configured in Stripe" claim is `UNVERIFIABLE-HERE` and names the check that would settle it.
- **Levels:** almost everything is L1 (static read, git history, `gh` PR/issue state). One command was run: the coverage-matrix checker. Its results count as L2 for "what the matrix says". The matrix's own Status cells are the builders' last recorded results, not a fresh run.
- **After the snapshot:** PR #279 (#183 items 2, 4, 9, 12, 13, 16, 18) merged 2026-11-22 UTC, after `523c633d`. It is not counted below.

- **Live and deploy state:** deployed functions, rules, indexes, hosting/AASA/assetlinks, PITR, the bucket, IAM grants, and staging `config/sales`. This task didn't authorize read-only gcloud/firebase/HTTP reads. Settled by: `gcloud functions list`, the rules release, GETs of `https://regroup-app.com/.well-known/{apple-app-site-association,assetlinks.json}`, and `gcloud iam service-accounts get-iam-policy`.
- **Stripe live and sandbox:** prices, portal, webhook endpoints, Connect, retry settings. Needs the main session's read-only Stripe calls.
- **#183 item 1** (iOS form modals fixed before the merge). Not traced. #279 merged after the snapshot.
- **Whether the matrix's `fail` rows are still red.** No suite was re-run: payment suite and Maestro need emulators, which the brief says not to start.
- **The A2 backfill question.** Spec A2 vs regroup/CLAUDE.md "no backfill is needed": the owner decides.

## Ledger: Regroup e2e / test-plan docs vs code (RD-E2E-)

Run 2026-11-22, code at `origin/main` 523c633d (`/Users/marcusklein/dev/rp-210-merge`, detached). All rows are **L1** (static read of code, config and scripts). No tests, emulators, builds or external calls were run. Paths are relative to `regroup/` unless they start with `scripts/`, `.github/` or `docs/` (repo root).

**Totals: 114 claims.** 53 IMPLEMENTED, 24 CONTRADICTED, 18 STALE-DOC, 11 PARTIAL, 6 MISSING, 2 UNVERIFIABLE-HERE.

| Group                                            | Claims | IMPL | CONTRA | STALE | PARTIAL | MISSING | UNVERIF |

| A. mobile/docs e2e (8 docs)                      | 67     | 29   | 17     | 10    | 7       | 3       | 1       |
| B. docs/technical/mobile/e2e (15 Detox-era docs) | 33     | 17   | 6      | 7     | 2       | 0       | 1       |
| C. activity test plan + 2 UX docs                | 14     | 7    | 1      | 1     | 2       | 3       | 0       |

Facts used across rows (L1):

- Detox is gone. `mobile/package.json` has no detox dependency or config, there is no `mobile/.detoxrc.js`, and `mobile/e2e/` holds only `SECURITY-test-credentials.md` and `setup/{testAccounts,testActivities,testDisputes}.json`.
- There are 34 Maestro flows in `mobile/maestro/flows/`: 16 in `run-all.yaml`, plus 14 `device-*`, 2 `deep-link-invitation-*`, 2 `debug-*` and `rent-pay-full.yaml`. `maestro/config.yaml` runs `flows/*`.
- `run-flows.sh` SUITE (`maestro/scripts/run-flows.sh:50-67`) is the same 16 flows as `run-all.yaml`. It doesn't include `rent-pay-full`.
- Rent payment in the app uses the Stripe PaymentSheet (`mobile/src/screens/RentPayment/RentPaymentScreen.tsx:83-166`). `PaymentWebView` is still registered as a route (`mobile/src/navigation/navigators.tsx:469`), but nothing navigates to it.
- Fixture privacy: `e2e/setup/testAccounts.json:2` says no passwords are stored there. Fixture phones are fictional 555 numbers, emails are on the test domain, and no fixture holds a password.

### B. regroup/docs/technical/mobile/e2e (Detox era, Feb 2026)

All 15 carry the same banner (line 3): "Superseded (2026-11-14): … Detox and its `e2e/tests/` suite are gone; Regroup e2e runs on Maestro only, against the E2EEmulator build", with links to `mobile/maestro/README.md` and `mobile/docs/e2e/coverage-matrix.md`. Both link targets exist. The banner claim is IMPLEMENTED at L1 for every file: no detox in mobile/package.json, no `.detoxrc.js`, no `mobile/e2e/tests/`. It is recorded once per file below as row "a". The bodies are snapshots; only claims that still matter are recorded.

### Candidates for the main session (re-verify)

None of these blocks the bar (a live card charge) by itself: these are test and harness docs. Candidates for MUST-FIX doc or harness work:

1. **RD-E2E-023 / -024 (ENG, doc, L1).** `mobile/docs/e2e/regroup-e2e-test-plan.md` has no superseded banner. It gives production app ids as `${APP_ID}` and prescribes a "prod smoke run (real backend, dedicated test account)", which is the exact path the guard exists to block (a production launch signs in anonymously against phoenix-cleanhouse). Add a banner or correct the doc.
2. **RD-E2E-039 / -038 / -027 (ENG, doc and scripts, L1).** `maestro/flows/rent-pay-full.yaml` is the only flow that completes an in-app rent payment through the PaymentSheet. No run doc in this set mentions it, and it's in neither `run-all.yaml` nor run-flows.sh SUITE. Meanwhile `npm run maestro:ios` / `maestro:android` sweep the whole directory (34 flows) and run it unannounced, with its once-per-UTC-day seed constraint. Anyone running the launch payment e2e from these docs either never exercises in-app payment completion or runs it by accident.
3. **RD-E2E-036 / -056 (ENG, doc, L1).** Both run docs name the old E2E-iPhone UDID as the default. run-flows.sh now requires a sim named `regroup-e2e` made by `regroup/e2e/scripts/provision-simulators.sh` (#176).
4. **RD-E2E-086 (OPS/ENG, privacy, L1).** A literal password string remains in tracked `docs/technical/mobile/e2e/E2E_TESTING_GUIDE.md:499`. The SECURITY note claims every literal is gone. Confirm it isn't the rotated shared value and replace it with `$E2E_TEST_PASSWORD`.
5. **RD-E2E-013 / -017 (ENG, doc, L1).** The XCODE26 handoff banner gives the wrong Stripe RN version (0.44.0, but it is 0.83.0). Its body tells readers to use `GCLOUD_PROJECT=phoenix-cleanhouse` with assert-firestore. Low risk (the script forces the emulator), but wrong.

### Open questions

(Needed to decide whether the operator manual-payment e2e can run at all.) Settle at L4/L5 with a read-only test-mode account lookup by the main session.
- **OPEN QUESTION —** Was the Sentry user token printed in the June session rotated (RD-E2E-019)? (Needed to decide whether to close the security note.) Settle in the Sentry console (OPS).
- Not settled here: whether any flow passes today (L3; no emulators or simulator were run), and whether the build guard and fail-closed emulator wiring behave on a device. Everything above is L1.

## Area: Deploy and config — regroup + recovery-api (reverse pass, 2026-11-21/22)

Code read at `523c633d` (worktree `rp-210-merge`). **`origin/main` moved to `2de821e5` during the run** (#279 merged 2026-11-22T05:04Z; it touches only mobile, `firestore.rules` and `firestore.indexes.json`, no functions source). Read with `gh api .../contents?ref=2de821e5`.

Live reads, all read-only, 2026-11-22 ~04:55–05:25Z: `gcloud functions list/describe` (env var **names** only, plus the values of `TIER_BILLING_ENABLED`, `CONNECT_ACCOUNTS_API` and `STRIPE_WEBHOOK_MODE`), the firebaserules REST API (releases and rulesets), `npx firebase-tools@15.37.0 firestore:indexes`, `gcloud secrets list` and `secrets versions list` (metadata only, no values), and the Hosting REST API (latest release and custom domains). I read no Firestore documents, Auth users or Storage objects, accessed no secret values and made no Stripe calls. The raw evidence is in `.../scratchpad/lrp/dep/` (describe JSON with env values redacted, rulesets, index dumps, CSVs). The scripts are in `.../scratchpad/lrp/dep-scripts/`.

**Staging changed during this run.** At about 05:16–05:18Z on 2026-11-22, someone deployed all 64 regroup functions, both rulesets and the indexes to `regroup-e2e`, consistent with `main` at `2de821e5`. The first snapshot (before) and the final one (after) are both recorded below.

Project ids: production `phoenix-cleanhouse`, staging `regroup-e2e`, recovery-api `recovery-platform-e6cb3` (`recovery-api/.firebaserc:3`). `recovery-platform` isn't a project this account can see (`gcloud projects list`), so the brief's id is wrong and the root CLAUDE.md's id is right.

### What's live vs main

**phoenix-cleanhouse (production): about 11 days behind `main`. Functions date from 2026-11-11/12, rules from 2026-11-11, Hosting/SSR from 2026-10-07.**

- **Functions.** 63 deployed; 64 endpoints committed. Missing: `getDocumentLink` and `resolveFailedPaymentOffline`.
  - The bulk deploy ran 2026-11-11 03:45–03:47Z. That's `main` up to `58ce2faf` (#242, merged 03:32Z).
  - `createOperatorSubscription` was redeployed at 06:41Z (#247).
  - `notify`, `onGuestWrite` and `rentCharges` were redeployed 2026-11-12 01:48Z (#253).
  - **Not live:** #230, #243, #246, **#254 (P4a: webhooks follow Stripe, grace from the invoice, sales and paywall switches)**, #259, #160 (with #232, #233 and #250), #267 and #275.
- **Firestore rules.** Equal to #229's file (`b7698cc3`), released 2026-11-11T03:49Z. Missing:
  - P4a's `paywall/config` read rule;
  - #160's rules: `choreRotations`, superAdmin equality, guest delete only by a higher rank, and a documents create may not set `storageUrl`;
  - #279's admins rule.
- **Storage rules.** Still the 2026-10-02 file (`78cbbf85`), with no documents paths.
- **Indexes.** 27 of 29: the two `drug-tests` indexes (#231) and the two `chat` indexes (#279) are missing. Two legacy `na-meetings` `gehoash` indexes are live but not committed.
- **Hosting/SSR.** 2026-10-07 build, with only the `** → ssr` rewrite: no `/connect/*` rewrites (#91).
- **Env.**
  - `TIER_BILLING_ENABLED=true`.
  - `CONNECT_ACCOUNTS_API` is unset, so new accounts are v1.
  - **`CONNECT_BASE_URL` is unset.**

**regroup-e2e (staging).**

- **Before the deploy during this run:** functions dated 2026-11-10 04:14Z, which is `main` at #212. Firestore rules were #212's (behind production: no #229 rent-money rules). Storage was the 2026-10-02 file. Three indexes were missing.
- **After 05:18Z:** functions, both rulesets and all 29 indexes match `main` at `2de821e5` (rules differ only by a trailing newline). The Oct-2 storage file was replaced by `main`'s.
- Hosting is the 2026-11-07 P3a build, with the `/connect` rewrites. `CONNECT_BASE_URL`, `ALLOWED_RETURN_ORIGINS`, `CONNECT_ACCOUNTS_API=v1` and `TIER_BILLING_ENABLED=true` are set.
- The two functions created by this deploy (`getDocumentLink`, `resolveFailedPaymentOffline`) have **no** custom env vars, because the deploy loaded no `functions/.env*`.

**recovery-platform-e6cb3 (recovery-api): current.**

- 7 of 7 exports are deployed. `findMeetings` dates from 2026-11-09 21:11Z (#207 merged at 21:08Z). The other six date from 2026-09-11; their only change since is #72's server `createdAt` on referrals.
- Indexes match, 2 of 2. There is no rules release (Admin SDK only). Both secrets exist.
- The upload excludes `src`, and neither checkout holds a `.env`.

### New operator, unaided (production as deployed today)

1. **Starts in the app.** Landing → "manager" → Login → "Sign up" → generic form → role-less account → endless spinner. **broken** (AU-03, AU-04; L1).
2. **Starts on the web.** `/pricing` loads (L5 GET). The tiers render in the browser from `getTierCatalog`. **unknown** (prod key is for the wrong Stripe account, MS-01).
3. **Web signup with a new email.** **works** (L1). An existing email is refused. **broken** for anyone who already has an account (AU-02).
4. **Card checkout** (`createOperatorSubscription`). **broken on prod** (MS-01, price lookup). With correct config: 7-day trial, `trialing`, works (L1, AU-01).
5. **`potentialSuperAdmin` claim.** **works** (L1). Nothing reads it (AU-09).
6. **Success page.** Says "Download the app", with no links. **friction** (AU-24). The store's current app is the old build. **unknown**.
7. **Mobile sign-in → setup** (new build). **works** (L1, AU-05).
8. **Plan check → wizard → `createHouses` → claims → token refresh → flags.** **works** (L1, AU-07). It is deployed (DEP-02).
9. **Gate on a trialing house.** **works** (L1).
10. **Invites from the wizard.** The email sends (deliverability **unknown**, AU-15), but the link is **broken** (AU-10, AU-11).
11. **Connect payout onboarding.** **broken on prod** (AU-22, DEP-13, DEP-14).
12. **Later lapse (cancel).** No way to resubscribe. **broken** (AU-02, AU-20).

### New resident via invite (production as deployed today)

1. **The operator sends an invitation** (`createInvitation`: tier, entitlement and capacity checks). **works** (L1, AU-13).
2. **The email arrives.** **unknown** (AU-15; no DMARC).
3. **The resident taps `https://regroup-app.com/redirect?url=com.rats.dev%3A…`.**
   - Not installed: 404 page, no store link, token lost. **broken** (L5 GET, AU-10, AU-14).
   - iOS, installed: AASA `/*` opens the app and the invitation is dropped. **broken** (L1 plus L5 AASA; device unconfirmed, AU-11).
   - Android, installed: assetlinks doesn't cover `com.regroup.app`, so the browser shows the 404. **broken** (L5 assetlinks, AU-11, ST-08).
4. **Fallbacks.** There is no invite code in the app and no redeem at login (AU-12). Applying through house search is self-signup, which ends on the endless spinner (AU-04, ST-22). **broken**.
5. **If a raw `regroup-app://?type=invitation&token=…` reaches the app** (only by hand): Splash → Signup, `peekInvitation` pre-fill → `createUser` → `redeemInvitation` → refresh → `createGuest` → NewAccount → Main. **works** (L1; new build).
6. **Gate on an active house.** **works** (L1).
7. **Pay rent.** Needs the house's Connect account. **broken on prod** (AU-22).
8. **An invitee who already has an account.** Can't redeem. **broken** (AU-12).

### Couldn't settle here

- iOS behavior when the app opens its own universal-link URL (ST-07, AU-20). Settle with a signed device build.
- Invite email deliverability (AU-15). Settle with a staging test send.
- Whether the demo account exists and is entitled (ST-21). Needs a prod Auth/Firestore read with owner approval.
- App Privacy, Data safety, age rating, the live store build numbers and the Play listing status (ST-16, ST-19, ST-23, ST-24, ST-25). Settle in the consoles.
- Where the live AASA/assetlinks come from, and which key `C5:72…` is (ST-06, ST-08). Settle in the Firebase console's app registrations and the Play Console's app signing.
- iOS archive health on RN 0.88 (ST-02). Settle with an owner archive or TestFlight upload.

## Main-session verification (MS-)

_Source: run working file `main-findings.md`._

## Main-session findings (L5, read-only, owner approved live Stripe reads 2026-11-21)

### MS-01 — Production Stripe key belongs to the wrong Stripe account (BLOCKER candidate, OPS)

- Deployed `createOperatorSubscription` (phoenix-cleanhouse, updateTime 2026-11-11T06:41:58Z) binds
  secret `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY@3` (cmd: `gcloud functions describe createOperatorSubscription --region us-central1 --project phoenix-cleanhouse`).
- Versions of `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` in phoenix-cleanhouse: 1 (2026-04-15), 2 (2026-05-26), 3 (2026-06-06), all enabled. 3 is latest.
- Version 3 is a live **secret** key whose own account is `acct_1RNFH5ID2FGlm411`, dashboard display
  name **"Homegroups App"**, business URL https://regroup-app.com, created 2025-05-10, products "Group",
  "Group", no subscriptions, no webhook endpoints, no connected accounts (cmd: stripe accounts.retrieve(),
  webhookEndpoints.list, accounts.list, subscriptions.list, products.list with that key).
- Regroup's live account is `acct_1GfryQKsstp3aqoP` (regroup-app.com) per
  `regroup/scripts/stripe-product-values.md:112` and the launch design.
- All 12 deployed `STRIPE_PRICE_*` IDs (6 monthly, 6 annual, all `...Ksstp3aqoP...`) return
  "No such price" with that key, as does the committed monthly TRAD_STARTER
  `price_1TnBmGKsstp3aqoP5cUjb8Eo`.
- Consequence: every Stripe call from Regroup production goes to the Homegroups App account. Tier
  checkout fails at price retrieval; Connect accounts, customers and subscriptions would be created
  in the wrong account; webhooks from the Regroup account (signed with STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET) refer to
  objects this key can't read.
- The 2026-10-19 plan's "live monthly env IDs match live prices (L5)" can't be reproduced with the
  key production uses; that run must have used another credential. Treat it as wrong.
- Fix (OPS): add Regroup's live secret key (`acct_1GfryQKsstp3aqoP`) as a new version of
  `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY` in phoenix-cleanhouse, redeploy every function that binds it, and re-run this
  read (price retrieval) with the new version before launch. Also decide whether Homegroups' live key
  should be revoked from this project (a cross-product secret).

### MS-02 — Deployed monthly price IDs differ from the committed live price values (UNVERIFIABLE-HERE)

- Deployed monthly IDs are `price_1TkbS*` (e.g. TRAD_STARTER `price_1TkbSgKsstp3aqoPbxh5xWSn`); the
  committed table `regroup/scripts/stripe-product-values.md:30-46` lists monthly `price_1TnBm*`
  (e.g. `price_1TnBmGKsstp3aqoP5cUjb8Eo`). The annual IDs match the table.
- Which set exists live on acct_1GfryQKsstp3aqoP can't be checked here: no credential for that
  account is available to this run. Settle with a live read of both IDs on the Regroup account.
- `regroup/scripts/stripe-product-values.md:79` says the house and Oxford IDs "are not live IDs", and
  :104 says some exist in sandboxes but not in live mode.

### Deployed switches (createOperatorSubscription env, L5)

TIER_BILLING_ENABLED=true; STRIPE_API_VERSION=2026-01-28.clover; no CONNECT_ACCOUNTS_API (v1 default);
secret env STRIPE_SECRET_KEY@3, SENDGRID_API_KEY@1. Function last deployed 2026-11-11, before #160
(2026-11-14) and the later merges.

### MS-03 — Connect onboarding throws on production (main-session verified, L5 + L1) = DEP-13
- Production `connectStripeAccount` (updateTime 2026-11-11T03:45:54Z) env has no `CONNECT_BASE_URL` (cmd: gcloud functions describe ... names only); staging has it (updateTime 2026-11-22T05:16:24Z).
- `connectBaseUrl()` throws when unset (regroup/functions/src/util/connectUrls.ts:69-79, unchanged since 38223a86 on 2026-10-17, before the deploy).
- Mobile sends only `{houseId}` (regroup/mobile/src/screens/HouseSettings/StripeSettingsScreen.tsx:232-236), so payments.ts:755 calls connectReturnUrl() and throws.

### MS-04 — Live web serves the Angular app on the Connect return paths (L5) = DEP-14
- `curl https://regroup-app.com/connect/stripe-return` → 200 with `<app-root>` and the SSR title, not the stripeConnectReturn function. main's regroup/web/firebase.json has /connect/stripe-return → stripeConnectReturn and /connect/stripe-refresh → stripeConnectReauth above `**`.
